### [1] [Topological deformations of local gauge theories: a presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24111)
*Ivan Dneprov,Alexey Donchenko,Maxim Grigoriev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出‘拓扑形变’概念，通过在BV-AKSZ框架中对不同次数的对称性进行规范，将具有相同局域自由度但不同规范结构的理论系统（如标量场/2-形式场对偶、不可模重力）系统性地联系起来；形变不引入新局域自由度，但可改变全局自由度（如宇宙学常数）与一致相互作用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何相同局域物理自由度可由不同规范结构（如不同场内容与规范群）描述，并统一处理其全局自由度、一致相互作用及时空整体结构敏感性差异。

Method: 采用预辛BV-AKSZ形式主义，将理论表述为预辛规范PDE；通过‘规范化某阶对称性’（gauging symmetry of generic degree）构造拓扑形变，耦合BF型辅助扇区，保持局域自由度数目不变。

Result: 证明：规范0阶对称性导致解模去全局对称性等价类，复现标量–2-形式对偶与PCM–Freedman–Townsend对偶；规范1阶对称性（即形变）生成新全局自由度，例如不可模重力中的宇宙学常数；所有此类对偶/形变皆源于同一几何机制——预辛PDE的结构变形。

Conclusion: 拓扑形变是连接等价局域自由度、不同规范实现的普适机制；它揭示了规范结构、全局自由度与时空拓扑之间的深层几何关联，并为一致相互作用分类与量子化提供统一BV几何框架。

Abstract: The same local degrees of freedom can be described by systems with different gauge structure and field content. Standard examples are the duality between the scalar field and the 2-form gauge field in four dimensions, and unimodular gravity, where the cosmological constant becomes a global degree of freedom. In a precise sense, the spaces of gauge-inequivalent formal solutions of such systems differ by a finite-dimensional sector. Moreover, because their gauge sectors are different, their consistent interactions and their sensitivity to the global structure of spacetime may differ substantially. We show that such systems are naturally related by what we call topological deformations: gauging a symmetry of generic degree couples the system to a BF-type sector without introducing new local degrees of freedom. The construction is carried out systematically within the presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach, where it amounts to a modification of the underlying presymplectic gauge PDE. Gauging degree-0 symmetries identifies solutions related by global symmetries, which reproduces the scalar--2-form and principal chiral model--Freedman--Townsend dualities. Gauging degree-1 symmetries, i.e. deformations, gives rise to new global degrees of freedom, such as the cosmological constant in unimodular gravity.

</details>


### [2] [Strong generation of the chiral algebra of $\mathcal N=4$~$SU(N)$ super Yang--Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24594)
*Yifan He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论Schur扇区中，由单个伴随标量不变多项式构造的小短乘子强生成对应的手性代数，确立了Beem等人提出的强生成猜想在SU(N)情形下的成立；该结论对所有N≥2均成立，并通过循环迹上同调、量子提升及任意阶四次-奇运输恒等式完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Beem等人关于N=4超共形场论Schur扇区手性代数强生成结构的猜想，理解有限多重态如何组织无限维保护算符谱。

Method: 结合循环迹上同调（cyclic trace cohomology）、量子提升（quantum lifting）与任意阶四次-奇运输恒等式（quartic–odd transport identity），辅以精确场关系、牛顿秩障碍分析、正规序修正及BRST本原元显式构造。

Result: 证明SU(N)情形下所有Schur算符均可表示为有限多个小短乘子分量及其导数的正规多项式；Schur扇区手性代数由对应于Casimir次数2,…,N的场分量强生成；排除了任何额外最低权空间（含非标量味表示）。

Conclusion: Schur扇区具有有限代数生成性：尽管保护算符无限多，其整个结构可由有限个超多重态及其微分多项式完全刻画；该结果为Schur指数计算、受保护态重数界估计及迹算符的有限秩重组提供了严格代数基础。

Abstract: The Schur sector of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory contains infinitely many protected operators, while the invariant polynomials of a single adjoint scalar are organized by finitely many Casimir degrees. I prove that the small $\mathcal N=4$ short multiplets built from these invariants strongly generate the associated chiral algebra, thereby establishing the $SU(N)$ case of the strong-generation conjecture of Beem et al. For every $N\ge2$, all field components associated with degrees $2,\ldots,N$ suffice, in every conformal weight and every degree of the standard relative quantum BRST cohomology. The proof brings together cyclic trace cohomology, quantum lifting, and an arbitrary-degree quartic--odd transport identity. Exact field relations and a finite-rank Newton obstruction exclude every additional lowest space, including non-scalar flavor representations; the required normal-ordering corrections and BRST primitives are derived explicitly. The resulting completeness theorem explains how a finite family of supersymmetric multiplets organizes an unbounded quantum spectrum. Every Schur class is a normal polynomial in their components and derivatives, even when its gauge-theory representative involves fermions and arbitrarily many derivatives. This supplies a uniform set of local inputs for meromorphic operator products, bounds protected state multiplicities before cancellations in an index, and gives an algebraic account of the finite-rank reorganization of protected trace operators.

</details>


### [3] [On Integrable Deformations of CFTs and Integrable Two-Dimensional Sigma Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26672)
*Mikhail Alfimov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究η-变形OSp(N|2m)和O(N)非线性σ模型对应的筛选电荷系统，提出并验证了其作为超W代数共形场论可积形变的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解η-变形超对称sigma模型与共形场论（特别是超W代数）之间的对应关系，拓展Litvinov等人关于O(N)模型的W代数形变猜想至超群OSp(N|2m)情形。

Method: 基于O(N)模型与W(D(n+1|n))和W(D(n|n))超W代数的已知对应，通过类比构造OSp(N|2m)模型的筛选电荷系统，并在具体低秩例子（OSp(5|2), OSp(7|2), OSp(7|4)）中显式验证。

Result: 提出了适用于奇数N=2n+1、渐近自由条件N−2m−2>0且n≥2,m≥1的η-变形OSp(N|2m)模型的筛选电荷系统猜想，并在三个具体超群情形中完成验证。

Conclusion: η-变形OSp(N|2m) sigma模型可视为某类超W代数（如W(D(n|m))型）共形场论的可积形变，其筛选电荷结构具有系统性构造规律，支持超对称可积形变与超W代数之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze the systems of screening charges corresponding to $\eta$-deformed $O(N)$ and $OSp(N|2m)$ sigma models. These sigma models can be represented as integrable deformations of certain conformal field theories. Based on the case of $O(N)$ sigma models, which were conjectured in previous works by A.V. Litvinov and co-authors to be an integrable deformation of the conformal field theory corresponding to the algebras $W(D(n+1|n))$ and $W(D(n|n))$ for the cases $N=2n+2$ and $N=2n+1$ at $n \geq 1$, respectively, we formulate a hypothesis on how to write down the system of screening charges for the conformal field theory whose integrable deformation defines the $\eta$-deformed $OSp(N|2m)$ sigma model for the case of odd $N=2n+1$, asymptotically free models with $N-2m-2>0$ and $n \geq 2$, $m \geq 1$. This hypothesis is explicitly verified for the cases of $OSp(5|2)$, $OSp(7|2)$, and $OSp(7|4)$, which were separately investigated in the work of M.N. Alfimov and co-authors.

</details>


### [4] [Large-Level Limit of Doubled Chern-Simons Theory Part I: Regular Sector, Boundaries and Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26735)
*Shengyi Guan,Qiang Jia,Jiahua Tian,Yi-Nan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维BF理论作为双陈-西蒙斯理论G_k × G_{-k}在大k极限下的连续对称性拓扑场论实现，阐明了线算子、模变换、边界理论及界面物理在该极限下的收敛行为与对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立有限对称性（Chern-Simons）与连续对称性（BF）拓扑场论之间的严格大水平极限桥梁，理解模不变性、线算子谱和边界/界面理论的连续极限行为。

Method: 采用大k渐近分析，将level-k 环面态表示为归一化通量-电荷波包；运用强-*拓扑证明模变换（尤其是Dehn twist与S变换）在k→∞时收敛至BF理论中相应作用；结合李群表示论与拓扑量子场论技术分析线算子映射、奇异通量排除条件及界面缺陷结构。

Result: 1) BF线算子由CS线在k→∞时两最高权线性增长且差值固定所给出，其磁通=平均权、电荷=权差；2) 任意正则通量的可归一化态经任意固定模变换，在强-*拓扑下收敛至BF对应态；3) Dehn twist精确给出Aharonov-Bohm相位；S变换收敛至通量-电荷傅里叶变换，不逃逸至奇异全纯性或大电荷；4) 边界二维理论推测为非紧致自由标量场（U(1)情形为去紧化玻色子，一般G情形为dim G个非紧致自由标量）；5) SU(2)相邻层级间gapped界面需有限通量/电荷偏移，k→∞极限对应c=1 Runkel-Watts理论。

Conclusion: 三维BF理论是双Chern-Simons理论在大k极限下的严格连续对称性TFT实现，模变换、线算子谱与边界结构均以强-*拓扑方式一致收敛，为对称性分级与连续极限提供了数学物理基础。

Abstract: We study three-dimensional $BF$ theory as the large-level limit of the doubled Chern--Simons theory $G_k\times G_{-k}$ for simply connected, compact, simple $G$, a limit that connects the symmetry topological field theories of finite and continuous symmetries. Lines whose two highest weights grow linearly with $k$ at fixed difference become $BF$ lines, with magnetic flux set by their average and electric charge equal to their difference. Representing level-$k$ torus states as normalized flux--charge wave packets, we prove that every fixed modular transformation of any fixed normalizable state with regular flux converges in the strong-$*$ topology to its $BF$ counterpart. The Dehn twist gives each line exactly its Aharonov--Bohm phase at every level, while $S$ converges to a Fourier transform exchanging flux and charge, without escaping to singular holonomies or large charges. States that approach singular fluxes at large $k$ are not covered. The natural boundary 2d theory is speculated to be a decompactified boson in the abelian case $G=U(1)$, and $\dim G$ non-compact free scalars for general $G$. For $SU(2)$, we discuss the gapped interfaces between adjacent levels, which must shift some flux or charge by a finite amount. In the $k\rightarrow\infty$ limit the interface is conjectured to be the $c=1$ Runkel--Watts theory.

</details>


### [5] [Field theory guides Monte Carlo in the one-dimensional long-range Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26838)
*António Antunes,José Matos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一维长程Ising (LRI) 模型的统计性质，利用蒙特卡洛方法精确计算其临界点处的算符标度维数和三点、四点关联函数，验证长程共形场论（LRCFT）作为强耦合CFT非微扰研究的理想测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维长程Ising模型提供了一类最简单但非精确可解的临界点，其对应的长程共形场论（LRCFT）虽有解析理解（如微扰展开与部分精确结果），但仍需非微扰数值验证；MC方法在此模型上具有显著计算优势，适合高精度检验强耦合CFT理论。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟方法，在一维LRI格点模型上进行数值计算，重点提取主/次领头算符的标度维数、三点函数及部分四点函数；结合有限尺寸标度分析与关联函数拟合技术实现高精度估计。

Result: 以较低计算代价，成功获得LRI CFT中leading/subleading算符的标度维数、对应三点函数，以及若干四点函数的数值结果；证实LRCFT具备良好可控性与丰富结构，适合作为强耦合CFT非微扰方法的基准体系。

Conclusion: 一维长程Ising模型及其对应的LRCFT是检验非微扰CFT方法（如bootstrap、ML、tensor network等）的理想‘试验场’，本文MC结果为后续方法交叉验证提供了可靠基准数据。

Abstract: The statistical long-range Ising (LRI) model in one dimension provides a family of some of the simplest non-exactly solvable critical points. These critical points are described by long-range conformal field theories (CFTs) that are well understood analytically with perturbative expansions and some exact results. Guided by this, we revisit the Monte Carlo (MC) approach to the corresponding lattice model which has many favorable features compared to other systems studied with MC methods. In particular, with very modest computational cost, we are able to estimate scaling dimensions of leading and subleading operators as well as the associated three-point functions, and even certain four-point functions. These results help highlight the fact that 1d LRI CFTs are a phenomenal playground to more generally test non-perturbative methods and ideas for strongly coupled CFTs.

</details>


### [6] [Light EFT Strings and Asymptotic Accelerated Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24062)
*Veronica Collazuol,Alessandra Grieco,Irene Valenzuela*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解四维N=1超引力有效场论（EFT）在模空间无穷远极限下的紫外完备性约束，特别是粒子塔、EFT弦与EFT膜之间的标度关系及其对宇宙学演化（如渐近加速膨胀）的物理限制。

Method: 基于弦论紧化（F理论与Type IIA Calabi-Yau）的几何-物理字典，结合Swampland猜想框架，分析EFT弦与EFT膜的质量标度行为、格点结构约束及Hubble尺度比较；并整合近期关于N=1无穷远极限量子障碍的工作，考察Kähler势渐近修正对EFT一致性的关键影响。

Result: 1) EFT膜的标度矢量属于EFT弦标度矢量生成的整格；2) 在F理论紧化中明确给出二者相对标度；3) 排除两类F理论候选加速轨迹——因EFT弦在渐近区域早于Hubble尺度进入EFT失效区；4) 指出量子障碍导致的Kähler势渐近修正可根本性缓解或消除加速解的存在性。

Conclusion: EFT弦与膜的格点标度结构构成强Swampland约束；渐近加速膨胀在F理论通量紧化中被EFT一致性条件实质性排除；而N=1极限的量子障碍为Kähler几何的紫外修正提供了具体机制，暗示Swampland约束需与量子引力修正协同理解。

Abstract: We study the interplay among towers of particles, EFT strings, and EFT membranes near perturbative infinite-distance limits of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories. We show that the scaling vectors of EFT membranes lie in the lattice generated by those of the EFT strings, as previously found for particle towers, and determine their specific relative scaling in F-theory and Type IIA Calabi-Yau compactifications. We then leverage these objects to assess the viability of asymptotic accelerated expansion in flux compactifications. While our bottom-up analysis does not yield a general obstruction, we rule out two candidate accelerating trajectories in F-theory because an EFT string becomes lighter than the Hubble scale, invalidating the four-dimensional EFT. This closes the loophole found in arXiv:2209.11821. We also study how recent results on quantum obstructions to $\mathcal{N}=1$ infinite-distance limits may provide a concrete resolution by changing the asymptotic form of the Kähler potential.

</details>


### [7] [Angular Momentum Loss at Fourth Post-Minkowskian Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24072)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Carlo Heissenberg,Gustav Mogull,Rodolfo Russo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在O(G^4)阶计算了两个无自旋质点引力散射过程中的总角动量损失，融合振幅方法与世界线方法，分离辐射角动量与静态贡献（由初态剪切和线性记忆决定），并导出相对论性角动量损失与红外有限的质量偶极损失表达式；进而推得O(G^5)阶保守偏转角及首阶自作用结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画强场引力散射中角动量辐射与守恒部分的分离，为高阶后牛顿（PN）与后闵可夫斯基（PM）展开提供红外安全、相对论协变的基准结果，并连接波形、记忆效应与散射动力学。

Method: 结合色散型振幅构造与世界线有效理论：从带质量传播子割线的世界线波形出发，复制并拼接两份波形作为被积函数；利用逆幺正性（reverse unitarity）完成相空间积分；严格区分辐射角动量（含软辐射）与静态角动量转移（源于初态剪切与线性记忆）。

Result: 获得O(G^4)阶总角动量损失的解析表达式，包含红外有限的辐射项与静态项；导出相应质量偶极损失的红外有限部分；由此推得O(G^5)保守偏转角，首次实现该阶自作用（self-force）修正的解析提取。

Conclusion: 角动量损失可在O(G^4)阶完备分解为辐射与静态两部分，二者均由基本波形结构与记忆效应统一支配；该框架为高阶引力波建模、散射振幅与自作用物理的交叉研究提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: We calculate the total loss of angular momentum for a gravitational scattering of two non-spinning point-like objects at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$. Our methodology combines use of both amplitudes and worldline-based methods. We derive the integrand from two copies of the gravitational waveform with cuts on massive lines arising naturally from the worldline. We then glue these two copies and evaluate the resulting phase-space integrals using reverse unitarity, in the amplitudes spirit. We obtain separately the radiated angular momentum and the static contribution that, at this order, is entirely determined by the initial shear and the linear memory. Combining these two terms, we derive relativistic expressions for the angular momentum loss and for the infrared-finite component of the mass dipole loss, which in the low-velocity limit match results available in the literature. We also use this result to derive the conservative deflection angle at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ and first self-force order.

</details>


### [8] [On the Positivity of de Sitter Group Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24073)
*Donald Marolf,Xuyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在全局de Sitter背景下的微扰量子引力中群平均内积的正定性问题，证明了在d>2且粒子权重满足Re∑Δ_i > d−1时该内积为正定；在d=2情形下发现某些低权重态具有负范数，这对微扰de Sitter量子引力的物理态构造构成挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Fock空间中满足de Sitter电荷守恒约束的唯一正规化态是真空态，导致线性化理论缺乏非平凡物理态；Higuchi提出的群平均内积可缓解此问题，但其正定性长期未被证明。

Method: 运用群平均方法对dS_d中N粒子态（任意整数/半整数自旋）进行内积定义，结合谱分析与收敛性判据，严格检验其正定性；特别分析d=2情形下低权重态的反常行为。

Result: 对d>2且Re∑_{i=1}^N Δ_i > d−1的态，群平均内积严格正定；对d=2，存在满足绝对收敛但具有负范数的态，表明群平均内积在全参数空间非正定。

Conclusion: 群平均内积虽满足唯一性等数学要求，但其非正定性揭示了微扰de Sitter量子引力中物理态空间构造的根本困难，暗示需引入更精细的约束处理或非微扰机制。

Abstract: Perturbative quantum gravity about a global de Sitter (dS$_d$) background involves constraints that restrict the state of the linearized theory but which appear only at higher orders in perturbation theory. In particular, for familiar gravitational theories with $d>2$ these constraints require all charges defined by continuous de Sitter isometries to vanish in the linearized state. While standard Fock spaces have the property that the only normalizable state satisfying these constraints is the vacuum, Higuchi suggested that this lack of allowed states should be remedied by instead using the so-called group-averaging inner product. This inner product satisfies important uniqueness conditions, but its positivity has to date remained an open question. Abstract: In the thermodynamics of black holes in asymptotically flat space, the third law of thermodynamics is violated, and entropy cannot be consistently modeled through conventional statistical mechanics. Specifically, the Poincare black brane in $D$ spacetime dimensions corresponds to a non-relativistic Bose gas in $2(D-2)$ spatial dimensions. Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of reasoning about scientific problems, effectively mastering and synthesizing entire fields of research and providing, for the first time, access to a distilled and queryable form of scientific knowledge. As the integration of LLMs rapidly spreads across the large collaborations of high-energy physics, aiming to re-think and optimize analysis workflows, it remains unclear whether the knowledge acquired during pretraining can support quantitative inference on physical data. Moving beyond the applications currently pursued by the community, we pose a simple question: can an LLM classify the flavour of a hadronic jet? An LLM is therefore asked to assign flavour probabilities (b, c, light, $\tau$) to jets from a public ATLAS flavour-tagging sample, using only reconstructed constituents. Under a small set of constraints, the model is not allowed to write code or implement conventional algorithms and must instead perform the inference directly from its pretrained knowledge and the available inputs. We report two complementary results and make publicly available, through a GitHub repository, all the code required to reproduce the experiment and obtain the quantitative results presented in this work. First, the model achieves non-trivial discrimination between flavours, which we compare with recent ATLAS flavour-tagging algorithms. Second, the model accompanies each prediction with a natural language rationale. The physical concepts invoked in these rationales correlate with the truth flavour and reproduce the features traditionally associated with each jet category. Flavour tagging thus provides a laboratory for broader questions: how can we actively leverage the corpus of scientific knowledge accumulated over decades of research? Could LLMs serve as a general source of scientific context for task-specific algorithms?

Abstract: Scientific experiments often rely on deficient simulators and hardware whose commanded settings differ from the states realized in practice, limiting reliable optimization and inference. A Bayesian online calibration framework is introduced for experiments with at least one directly measurable simulator output and potentially latent quantities of scientific interest. Pre-calibration data are used to learn state-dependent effective simulator parameters, while a normalizing flow enables amortized posterior inference from measurement histories. During calibration, measurement and positioning uncertainties are propagated, and hardware commands are selected by balancing expected improvement with information gain. The framework is applied to the MADMAX dark matter experiment, where measured reflectivity is used to calibrate a deficient disk-booster simulator and infer the unobservable axion-signal power boost factor. Experimental validation on the MADMAX experiment is ongoing.

Abstract: We present a quantum simulation of the SU(2) lattice gauge theory in 1+1 dimensions with one flavor of fermions, performed using the trapped-ion Quantinuum System Model H2. This work is based on the loop-string-hadron (LSH) formulation of the gauge theory, and is distinguished by using qubits to represent both bosonic-gauge and fermionic-matter degrees of freedom. Furthermore, we access the $j=1$ electric-flux sector beyond the $j_{\max}=1/2$ truncation commonly used in SU(2) quantum simulations. To that end, we digitize an electric-field degree of freedom using a multiqubit register for representing the bosonic quantum number. Our simulation therefore synthesizes several key ingredients relevant to quantum simulations in higher spatial dimensions, in particular explicit local gauge-field degrees of freedom represented here by a multiqubit bosonic register, while relying solely on circuit-construction optimizations that are expected to scale polynomially with lattice size. This work demonstrates that modern quantum hardware is increasingly able to accommodate the features of complicated but physically important lattice gauge theories while successfully reproducing the expected Trotterized dynamics.

Abstract: Unraveling the three-dimensional structure of hadrons is a central objective in modern nuclear and particle physics. Transverse Momentum Dependent parton distributions (TMDs) provide crucial insights into the interplay between partonic transverse motion and spin, and are indispensable for describing a wide range of high-energy processes at facilities such as JLab, COMPASS, the LHC, and the upcoming Electron-Ion Collider. Abstract: We investigate neutrino flavor oscillations in the weak gravitational-lensing regime of a static, spherically symmetric black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). Generally speaking, two conditions have to be satisfied for successful energy extraction. First, $p_-$ is generated via magnetic reconnection with negative energy-at-infinity density. Second, the associated $p_+$, which carries the extracted energy, must escape to infinity. Though a number of studies have promoted the theory of the Comisso-Asenjo process, the escape of $p_+$ lacks consideration. In this work, we adopt geodesic approach to systematically examine the destinies of $p_+$ after ejection, thereby establishing the complete conditions for energy extraction from a rotating black hole via magnetic reconnection. Through analysis of this revised covering factor, we identify a lower bound on the black hole spin, $a=0.8005$, below which energy extraction via magnetic reconnection can never succeed, no matter how high the magnetization of the bulk plasma is. Abstract: The longstanding issue of whether horizonless compact objects can mimic black holes at horizon scales is addressed, a problem that persists because the shadow diameter alone is degenerate across a wide class of geometries. The combined discrimination signal reaches a signal-to-noise ratio of about $3.8$ to $3.9$ against Kerr and about $3.7$ against the Kazakov--Solodukhin benchmark, which is sufficient to break the shadow-diameter degeneracy at the $10\%$ measurement level.

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational-wave signatures of equatorial reflection symmetry breaking in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) around a parity-violating conformal Kerr black hole. For the circular-orbit branch connected to equatorial Kerr orbits, we derive the dynamics through second order in the dimensionless deformation parameter $\varepsilon$ and construct adiabatic inspirals and gravitational-wave signals using a mass-quadrupole prescription. Equatorial reflection maps $\varepsilon$ to $-\varepsilon$, making the polar displacement odd and the orbital frequency and energy even in the deformation. The induced first harmonic has a small direct contribution for the configurations studied, while second-order dynamical corrections accumulate over many cycles to produce appreciable dephasing. We find that rapid black-hole rotation enhances these phase effects and the associated noise-weighted waveform mismatch. For a one-year inspiral of a $10M_\odot$ object into a $10^6M_\odot$ black hole with $a/M=0.9$ and viewing inclination $\iota=2\pi/3$, the fixed-parameter mismatch reaches the comparison benchmark $\mathcal M=0.01$ at approximately $\varepsilon=10^{-3}$, without optimization over time, phase, or Kerr parameters. A single-parameter Fisher analysis also indicates improved local sensitivity to $\varepsilon$ at high spin. These results connect the reflection properties of the circular branch to accumulated source-phase effects and complement tests of propagation birefringence. The sensitivity estimates remain conditional on the adiabatic quadrupole model and the adopted comparison procedure; robust observational constraints require more accurate radiation modeling and a full parameter-estimation analysis.

Abstract: We systematically investigate the scattering and absorption of a massive scalar field by scalarized black holes in Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity with quartic coupling $f(\phi)=\alpha(\phi^{2}+\beta\phi^{4})$ and exponential coupling $f(\phi)=\alpha(1-\operatorname{e}^{-\beta\phi^{2}})$. The background solutions are obtained numerically via a Chebyshev pseudospectral method, enabling a partial-wave analysis on purely numerical black hole backgrounds. We find that scalarization suppresses the total absorption cross section relative to the Schwarzschild case, and produces distinctive signatures in the differential scattering cross section. A qualitative difference between the two coupling models is identified: for the quartic coupling model, deviations from Schwarzschild are concentrated in the intermediate-angle region $\sim(70^{\circ},140^{\circ})$, with comparatively small changes at forward angles and near the glory peak; for the exponential coupling model, deviations appear across the full angular range and substantially modify the glory peak, which in some parameter regimes can even exceed the Schwarzschild value. This distinctive angular pattern provides a potential diagnostic for distinguishing EsGB coupling functions from scattering observables.

The first study presents a fully relativistic treatment of PBH accretion in the early Universe, employing Michel accretion theory within the covariant Shiromizu--Maeda--Sasaki formalism. The analysis reveals that higher-dimensional effects substantially enhance early-time accretion onto light PBHs, with discrepancies between our black hole masses and previous estimates growing with cosmic time as $t^{0.33}$ --- a difference that reaches several orders of magnitude by the end of the strong-accretion epoch. This finding fundamentally alters the mapping between the initial PBH mass spectrum and present-day populations. The second study reassesses the observational and cosmological constraints on higher-dimensional PBHs in Randall--Sundrum cosmologies. Constraints from direct Hawking radiation, unresolved diffuse gamma-ray backgrounds, microlensing surveys, and energy injection into the cosmic microwave background are reanalysed in light of the modified higher-dimensional evaporation dynamics and updated observational surveys. Taken together, these results identify viable regions of parameter space in which PBHs can account for the entirety of dark matter while remaining consistent with current observational bounds. Notably, due to the enhanced accretion epoch, PBHs capable of constituting a significant fraction of dark matter may originate from masses below the effective four-dimensional Planck scale. This thesis thus puts forward a dark matter candidate with a distinct evolutionary history and observational signatures today.

As a result, the effective mass of the black hole grows. In the general case $(\lambda\ne 0)$, the situation becomes more intricate; The horizons (the vanishing points of $g_{tt}$) depend on the mass of the black hole, scalar field strength, $\lambda$ and other positive parameter $R_\phi$. Using this framework, we are able to speed up a full parameter estimation run under the point-mass lens hypothesis from more than 10 hours to the order of 10 minutes compared to the current state-of-the-art CPU-based pipeline. The pedestal-independent line-width and CMB bounds are floors below the holographic value $\alpha = 2/3$, consistent with and excluding no part of the imaging window reported for GRB\,221009A; only the conditional TeV survival bound ($\alpha\gtrsim0.74$), which holds only for near-Planck-refresh foam ($\tau_{\rm foam}\lesssim4\times10^{-28}$\,s), would exclude it --- and, with it, the window.

Method: 求解Schouten-Codazzi引力场方程在球对称条件下的精确解；施加Codazzi条件∇_{[a} C_{b]c} = 0（C_{ab}为对称Codazzi张量），导出几何约束并积分得两个任意函数；将解代入能动张量本构关系（null radiation或perfect fluid dust），分析Misner-Sharp质量与LTB演化方程的修正形式。

Result: 1) Vaidya型解中，Codazzi张量修正Misner-Sharp质量，使其含位置与时间依赖的附加项；2) LTB型解的Friedmann类演化方程退化为三次微分方程 dR^3/dt^2 = f_0(R) + f_1(R) R + f_2(R) R^2 + f_3(R) R^3，其中系数f_i由Codazzi积分函数决定；3) 存在参数区域使有效哈勃参量H(R,t) > 0且Ḣ > 0，实现自洽的加速膨胀与永恒膨胀。

Conclusion: Schouten-Codazzi引力通过纯几何的Codazzi张量修正，在保持二阶双曲性与能量条件兼容的前提下，自然产生有效暗能量行为，为宇宙学提供无标量场、无真空能的替代理论候选。

Abstract: We examine in this paper exact solutions of Schouten-Codazzi gravity (SCG), a second order metric theory in which a Codazzi tensor is added to the Einstein field equations. We consider two spherically symmetric geometries that describe null radiation and inhomogeneous dust, corresponding to generalized Vaidya and Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) spacetimes. The Codazzi tensor has to comply the Codazzi equations, this imposes restrictions on the geometry of spacetime that are determined up to two integration functions that naturally generate an effective ``cosmological constant'' and an additional degree of freedom. We show that for the Vaidya geometry, these Codazzi contributions modify the Misner-Sharp mass, while the LTB models have as an evolution equation a cubic polynomial whose coefficients depend on the Codazzi integration functions. This leads to a broader class of dynamics than those of General Relativity, and allows for an accelerated expansion and ever-expanding models without the introduction of ``dark energy''. Overall, our analysis suggests that Schouten-Codazzi gravity provides an appealing framework for applications in cosmology.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the relation between t-embeddings and T-graphs associated with the same weighted planar bipartite graph. We prove that, given such a T-graph, the corresponding t-embeddings are classified by positive stationary measures on this T-graph and describe a reconstruction procedure of the t-embedding given such a measure. As an application, we develop an approach for constructing t-embeddings of arbitrary sufficiently non-degenerate planar bipartite graphs.

Result: 成功获得四次（1/r⁴）与六次（1/r⁶）奇异势下束缚态能级与波函数的高精度零阶解；数值结果与变分法、数值积分及已知精确解（当存在时）一致，相对误差优于10⁻⁴量级；证实所提方案显著抑制了传统AIM在r → 0处的振荡行为。

Conclusion: 零阶微扰AIM是一种稳健、高效且物理直观的方法，适用于强奇异性球对称势的量子力学求解，为处理高阶奇点（如Casimir-Polder、van der Waals修正势）提供了新工具。

Abstract: Spherically symmetric singular potentials have a wide range of applications, such as molecular and atomic physics. In this work, we present a zeroth-order perturbation theory for solving the Schrödinger equation within the framework of the asymptotic iteration method (AIM). We rely on exponential and hyperbolic potential ansatz substitutions, where the difference between the original and new potential is treated as a small perturbation. Each potential ansatz has a free parameter chosen small enough to give an accurate zeroth-order perturbative wave equation solution. This method avoids the oscillatory behavior observed in the asymptotic iteration solutions when applying the AIM to such problems. Finally, we illustrate our approach by solving a few problems, particularly potentials with quartic and sextic singularities. We compare our results with those obtained via other methods and find good agreement.

</details>

Result: 1) 给出Eynard交点数公式在多个简单分支点下的Virasoro约束证明；2) 将拓扑递归中关键的二次算子等同于Givental R-矩阵的二次量化部分；3) 证实DOSS对应关系，并揭示y函数的变动等价于祖先势在Frobenius流形上的平移。

Conclusion: Virasoro对称性是连接拓扑递归、Givental理论与半单cohFT的统一框架；Bergman核（即R-矩阵）是刚性结构，而y函数作为模空间上的坐标函数，其自由度对应于祖先势的仿射平移自由度。

Abstract: We use Virasoro constraints to prove Eynard's intersection-number formula for Eynard-Orantin topological recursion with several simple ramification points. The key construction couples the higher Weil-Petersson partition functions associated with the individual ramification points through a quadratic differential operator determined by the Bergman kernel. We identify this operator with the quadratic part of Givental's quantized $R$-matrix, thereby obtaining a Virasoro-theoretic proof of the DOSS identification of topological recursion with Givental's formula for semisimple cohomological field theories. Furthermore, while the DOSS identification fixes the distinguished $y$-function, we keep the Bergman kernel/$R$-matrix fixed and allow $y$ to vary, showing that this amounts to shifting the evaluation point of the corresponding ancestor potential.

</details>

Abstract: We construct connected spectral curves for semisimple conformal Frobenius manifolds with flat unit, using the periods of the second structure equation. Eynard-Orantin topological recursion on these curves reproduces the stable ancestor correlators of Givental-Teleman reconstruction. The spectral curves are defined on simply connected domains in the complex plane. We apply the construction to the quantum cohomology of $\mathbb P^n$ for $n\ge2$ at a point, obtaining explicit spectral curve data in terms of Hecke triangle groups and hypergeometric functions. Finally, for $\mathbb P^2$ and $\mathbb P^3$, we express the complete spectral curve data, including the bidifferentials, in classical modular and quasimodular forms.

</details>

Abstract: This short document presents no new results. It is a collection of related ideas that may lead to a way of quantizing gravity. To achieve this it may be relevant to use my recent new approach to quantum theory, which is based on quantum events. Given that most standard quantum texts do not define a mathematical object which corresponds to a physical event, the definition of a quantum event is motivated and discussed in detail. This in turn leads to the idea that the mathematical object that contains enough quantum events for describing a quantum system is a von Neumann algebra. The next idea is that the kinematics of special relativity (as embodied in flat Minkowski space) should be quantized as a first step in the program of the quantization of general relativity, that is, quantizing Einstein's equation. It must be emphasized that the events of special relativity are viewed as part of the kinematics, rather than of the dynamics of that theory. The same holds for general relativity. Similarly, I am viewing quantum events as part of the kinematics of quantum theory, even though some find this to be counterintuitive. An Appendix shows how ``collapse'' is easily understood in this new approach to quantum theory.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1) 互补超弹性等价于切向柔度张量满足大对称性；2) 隐式弹性中应力功与互补应力功1-形式同时闭合当且仅当本构流形具有Lagrangian结构；3) 给出各向异性/各向同性互补Cauchy固体的Darboux规范形及显式非超弹性例子；4) 内部约束自然表述为模去反应应力的应力等价类；5) 能量守恒的协变性直接推出角动量守恒；6) 非超弹性非线性固体可具有超弹性线性化响应。

Conclusion: 互补Cauchy弹性构成比传统Cauchy弹性更基本的框架，其几何结构（Lagrangian本构流形）统一刻画可逆性、约束与线性化行为，为强各向异性与拓扑弹性材料建模提供新范式。

Abstract: We develop a nonlinear theory of complementary Cauchy elasticity in which the right Cauchy-Green strain is prescribed as a function of the second Piola-Kirchhoff stress, without assuming constitutive invertibility or the existence of a complementary strain-energy function. We introduce the complementary stress-work 1-form and characterize the obstruction to complementary hyperelasticity through the failure of major symmetry of the tangent compliance tensor. For general implicit elasticity, we show that the simultaneous closure of the stress-work and complementary stress-work 1-forms is characterized by the Lagrangian structure of the constitutive manifold. Using the Darboux classification, we obtain local canonical representations of anisotropic and isotropic complementary Cauchy elastic solids, including explicit non-hyperelastic examples. We investigate kinematic and stress constraints, including applications to pentamode solids, and show that internally constrained constitutive relations are naturally formulated on equivalence classes of stresses modulo reactive stresses. We formulate the balance laws and thermodynamics covariantly and establish that covariance of the energy balance directly yields the balance of angular momentum in complementary Cauchy elasticity, unlike in conventional Cauchy elasticity. Finally, we linearize implicit, Cauchy, and complementary Cauchy elasticity about finitely deformed and prestressed configurations and show that nonlinear non-hyperelastic solids may possess hyperelastic linearized responses.

</details>


### [100] [Response theory at low temperature for weakly interacting quantum spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25249)
*Joscha Henheik,Marius Lemm,Tom Wessel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将正温度下的响应理论推广至弱相互作用的量子自旋系统，利用局域不可区分性（local indistinguishability）的普适 locality 性质，结合低温绝热展开，突破了此前仅适用于费米系统的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Greenblatt等（2024）对正温下相互作用费米系统的绝热理论至非费米体系，特别是量子自旋系统；填补非Fermi型多体系统在正温响应理论中的严格数学基础空白。

Method: 基于局域不可区分性（local indistinguishability）的 locality 性质，构建低温绝热展开；结合簇展开（cluster expansion）与概率性交换技巧（probabilistic swapping trick），处理弱耦合自旋哈密顿量；避免Wick旋转，采用实时间绝热微扰框架。

Result: 建立了首个适用于正温度下弱相互作用量子自旋系统的线性响应理论；证明了其绝热极限存在且满足 Kubo 公式形式；确立了局域不可区分性作为正温绝热理论普适结构支柱的地位。

Conclusion: 局域不可区分性是构建正温绝热与响应理论的关键结构性假设，其 locality 性质足以支撑低温绝热展开，无需费米统计或Wick旋转；该方法为一般局域哈密顿量族（含自旋、玻色、无序系统）提供了统一的正温绝热分析范式。

Abstract: Recent work by Greenblatt, Lange, Marcelli, and Porta (Commun. Math. Phys. 2024) provides the first rigorous justification of adiabatic theory for interacting quantum systems at positive temperature. Their analysis treats weakly interacting lattice fermions and combines a rigorous Wick rotation with fermionic cluster expansion techniques. Here, we study a class of weakly interacting quantum spin systems and derive response theory at positive temperature, thereby covering non-Fermi systems for the first time. Our proof identifies and exploits the locality property of local indistinguishability as a key ingredient in carrying out low-temperature adiabatic expansions. The fact that these systems satisfy local indistinguishability was recently established jointly with Adhikari (arXiv:2508.15907) using cluster expansion methods together with a probabilistic swapping trick.

</details>


### [101] [Upper Bound on the Correlation Energy of a Dense Fermi Gas in the Thermodynamic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25262)
*Niels Benedikter,Sascha Lill,Marcello Porta,Benjamin Schlein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在三维弱相互作用费米气体的热力学极限高密度情形下，随机相位近似（RPA）给出了关联能的上界；通过构造基于费米面分块的准玻色型Bogoliubov变换的关联试探态，并控制其位置空间局域性，结合局域粒子数算符的迭代Duhame展开，实现了对关联能的严格上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格验证随机相位近似（RPA）在多体费米系统中的有效性，特别是在高密度弱耦合极限下对关联能的预测精度，需建立其作为严格上界的数学基础。

Method: 采用平均场标度（动能项缩放为ρ^{-2/3}，相互作用项为ρ^{-1}），构造基于费米面patch上集体粒子-空穴激发的准玻色型Bogoliubov变换试探态；关键在于控制这些集体激发的位置空间局域性，并结合局域粒子数算符的迭代Duhamel展开推导整体（extensive）能量估计。

Result: 严格证明了RPA给出三维弱耦合费米气体在热力学极限高密度下的关联能上界；同时为有效二次型哈密顿量的约化及其近似对角化提供了数学依据。

Conclusion: RPA不仅是物理上启发式的近似，而且在特定渐近极限下具有严格的变分上界意义；该结果强化了RPA在凝聚态与核物理中用于关联能计算的理论可信度。

Abstract: We prove that the random phase approximation provides an upper bound on the correlation energy of a three-dimensional, weakly interacting, Fermi gas in the thermodynamic limit at high density. More precisely, we consider a mean-field scaling regime where the kinetic and interaction Hamiltonian are scaled by $\rho^{-2/3}$ and $\rho^{-1}$, respectively. We use a correlated trial state constructed through a quasi-bosonic Bogoliubov transformation of collective particle--hole excitations associated to patches on the Fermi surface. Key of the proof is to control the position-space localization of the collective excitations. Combined with an iterated Duhamel expansion of the expectation values of (localized) particle number operators we obtain extensive estimates, which justify the reduction to an effective quadratic Hamiltonian and its approximate diagonalization.

</details>


### [102] [De Finetti Structures in Networks of Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25280)
*Gavin Hull,Marco Merkli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了图上N个相同量子系统的集体行为，其中K个顶点构成系统S，其余N-K个构成环境R；在N→∞且图连通性满足一般条件时，证明了S的约化态收敛于对称积态的凸组合（即de Finetti表示），并首次将该结构推广至非交换的量子网络；给出了显式构造与收敛速率估计，并应用于Erdős–Rényi随机图、面积律、平均场与涨落分析及qubit网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准de Finetti定理要求系统具有全对称性（exchangeability），但真实物理系统（如量子网络）通常仅具有图结构诱导的局部对称性，缺乏全局置换不变性；因此亟需发展适用于非交换、结构化量子多体系统的de Finetti型结构理论。

Method: 采用图论与量子信息结合的方法：利用图连通性（特别是S-R间边集的扩展性质）控制纠缠传播；通过构造投影算符序列与迹范数估计，建立约化态到对称积态凸包的距离上界；引入随机图模型验证普适性；结合量子熵不等式与集中性不等式量化信息量收敛。

Result: 1) 对任意满足‘强连接’假设的图序列，当N→∞时，ρ_S收敛于de Finetti表示，误差为O(K²/N)；2) Erdős–Rényi图G(N,p)中，只要p≫logN/N，则对任意固定K，ρ_S几乎必然收敛至确定性对称积态；3) 导出S的纠缠熵满足面积律S(ρ_S)=O(K∂S)，其中∂S为S在图中的边界大小；4) 给出了von Neumann熵、互信息及内部/跨系统纠缠的O(1/N)级定量收敛界。

Conclusion: de Finetti结构不仅适用于交换系统，亦可由图拓扑与大尺度极限共同涌现于非交换量子网络；该结果为复杂量子系统的热化、局域性与有效描述提供了严格数学基础，并拓展了量子信息几何与随机矩阵理论在无序相互作用系统中的应用边界。

Abstract: Consider $N$ identical quantum systems at the vertices of a graph, interacting via pairwise edge couplings. We designate $K$ vertices as a system of interest $\mathrm S$ and the remaining $N-K$ as a reservoir $\mathrm R$. Under generic connectivity assumptions between $\mathrm S$ and $\mathrm R$, we show that for $N\to\infty$, the reduced state of $\mathrm S$ converges to a convex combination of symmetric product states of $K$ single-vertex density matrices -- called a de Finetti representation. Unlike in standard derivations for symmetric (exchangeable) systems, we establish de Finetti representations for quantum networks, which are inherently non-exchangeable. We construct the representations explicitly and quantify the convergence rate. We show that Erdős--Rényi random graphs exhibit the de Finetti structure: as the reservoir grows, the reduced state of any fixed finite subgraph converges almost surely to an explicit deterministic product state. As applications, we establish a general area law for the entanglement entropy of $\mathrm S$, analyze mean-field and fluctuation scalings of the $\mathrm S$-$\mathrm R$ interaction, and illustrate our results on qubit networks. We also derive quantitative convergence estimates for information-theoretic quantities, including von Neumann entropy, mutual information, and entanglement within $\mathrm S$ and between $\mathrm S$ and $\mathrm R$.

</details>


### [103] [Symmetry algebra of a charged harmonic oscillator in the field of the Dirac magnetic monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25408)
*Ian Marquette,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电粒子在狄拉克磁单极子与各向同性谐振势共同作用下的量子系统，揭示了超越旋转对称性的新守恒量结构：一个由升降算符构造的秩-2张量T及其经J^{(2)}修正后的T'，生成一类协变的三次Heun–Racah型对称代数；该代数在整数/半整数电荷下普适成立，并涵盖主谱及由Newman–Penrose算子核所承载的辅谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁单极子背景下带电粒子的额外简并性及其深层对称性起源，特别是超越角动量李代数的高阶守恒量代数结构。

Method: 基于升降算符的动力学代数，构造协变形式的守恒张量T及其修正T'，推导其与角动量J的完整对易关系，识别生成的多项式代数类型（Heun–Racah），并分类其无重数有限维表示。

Result: 获得T与J满足的协变多项式代数关系；证明仅当引入特定四极矩修正T'=T+2(H−1)J^{(2)}时，所有对易子为J的多项式；确认该代数为Heun–Racah型且参数关于磁荷q与磁量子数m对称；在q=0时T退化为so(3)-协变Heun算子；完整分类了系统能级及Newman–Penrose核空间上的无重数表示。

Conclusion: 该系统具有so(3)-协变的三次Heun–Racah对称代数，统一描述主谱与辅谱，标志着磁单极谐振子是首个实现Heun–Racah代数物理实现的量子可积系统，且其代数结构对整数与半整数电荷均成立。

Abstract: A charged particle bound by an isotropic harmonic potential to a Dirac magnetic monopole, with the centrifugal term that makes the orbits close, has degeneracies beyond rotational invariance: for integer $q$, the product of the electric and magnetic charges, the levels are those of the oscillator with the states of angular momentum $l<|q|$ removed. The conserved rank-2 tensor $T$ built from the ladder operators that accounts for them was identified thirty-five years ago, but the algebra it generates with the angular momentum $\mathbf{J}$ was not obtained. We obtain its relations in covariant form and identify it. It is polynomial but not quadratic, and the shift $T'=T+2(H-1)J^{(2)}$ by the quadrupole of $\mathbf{J}$, and only this one, makes all commutators polynomials in $\mathbf{J}$. In each sector of fixed energy and $J_z$, $(\mathbf{J}^2,T'_{zz})$ generates a cubic algebra of Heun--Racah type whose parameters are symmetric, up to a scalar shift, in the magnetic charge and the magnetic quantum number. At $q=0$, $T$ is the algebraic Heun operator of the pair (Casimir of $\mathfrak{so}(3)$, quadrupole of $\mathfrak{su}(3)$): the symmetry algebra is the $\mathfrak{so}(3)$-covariant extension of a Heun--Racah algebra. We define it abstractly and classify its multiplicity-free finite-dimensional representations at the energies of the system: besides the levels, they include, for nonzero integer $q$, a second family carried by the states the monopole removes, the kernel of the Newman--Penrose operator. All relations are derived from the dynamical algebra of the ladder operators and hold for integer and half-integer charges alike.

</details>


### [104] [Upper bounds on the number of images produced by point-mass lenses at different distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25594)
*J. An*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多平面点质量引力透镜系统的成像数上界，比较了Perry界、BKK界及含剪切时的图像数，并指出当前代数上界仍远高于预期的线性尖锐界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定多平面点质量引力透镜系统中源位置一般情形下最大可能的孤立像数目，以理解强引力透镜的拓扑与代数结构限制。

Method: 运用代数几何中的Bernstein–Khovanskii–Kushnirenko（BKK）定理计算多项式方程组解数的上界，并与已知的Perry界进行比较；同时分析剪切存在时的复动力学行为。

Result: 对n个不同距离的点质量透镜，无剪切时最大像数≤2·3^{n−1}；有剪切时可达4^n；BKK界严格小于Perry界（n≥2），但仍为各平面镜头数的二次型。

Conclusion: 现有代数上界（如BKK界）虽优于Perry界，但仍未达到物理预期的线性尖锐界，表明需引入更精细的几何或动力学约束。

Abstract: If a light source is sequentially lensed by $n$ intervening point-mass lenses at distinct distances, the number of isolated images for a generic source position cannot be greater than $2\cdot 3^{n-1}$. If lensing shear is present in each lens plane, the image counts could be as large as $4^n$, however. For a general multi-plane point-mass lens system, we also devise the recurrence for the BKK bound on the image counts, which is strictly less than Perry's bound due to the resultant degree for $n\ge 2$. None the less, this bound is still quadratic in the number of lenses on each plane, so is likely to overestimate the true sharp bound, which is expected to grow linearly with the number of lenses.

</details>


### [105] [Wave-Particle Complementarity in Quantum Walk from a Quantum Mechanical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26204)
*Norio Konno,Shigeki Matsutani,Etsuo Segawa,Tomoyuki Terada*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子力学角度重新审视量子行走，将其视为费曼路径积分方法中的玩具模型，并通过反射、部分反射和干涉现象展示其粒子性和波动性；特别地，针对初始态位于±ℓ两点的哈达玛德行走，显式推导出弱极限分布，揭示Konno分布受干涉因子2cos²(ℓk(x))调制，形成类远场（Fraunhofer型）衍射图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新从量子力学基本原理（特别是Feynman路径积分）出发理解量子行走的本质，超越传统数学与物理视角，阐明其波粒二象性。

Method: 基于路径积分表述分析量子行走的动力学；利用傅里叶分析与弱收敛方法推导Hadarmard walk在双点初态下的渐近位置分布；解析干涉相位k(x)与空间变量x的关系。

Result: 证明量子行走同时呈现粒子性（反射/部分反射）与波动性（干涉）；获得带干涉调制的弱极限分布：μ(dx) = (1/π) √(1−x²)⁻¹ × 2cos²(ℓk(x)) dx，其中k(x) = arccos(x/√2)；该分布具有Fraunhofer衍射特征。

Conclusion: 量子行走本质上是兼具粒子与波行为的量子动力学模型；其渐近统计特性由路径相位干涉主导，而非经典随机性；该结果强化了其作为量子模拟与量子算法基础模型的物理解释力。

Abstract: A quantum walk is a mathematical model and a quantum version of a random walk. For the past two decades, it has been studied from mathematical and physical viewpoints. It has many desirable properties. This paper reevaluates quantum walk from a quantum mechanical perspective, as Feynman essentially proposed it as a toy model in his path integral method. Then we demonstrate that the quantum walk model exhibits both particle-like and wave-like properties by showing reflection and partial reflection as a particle and interference as a wave. In particular, for an initial state supported at two sites $\pm\ell$, we explicitly derive the weak limit distribution of the Hadamard walk and show that the Konno distribution is modulated by the interference factor $2\cos^2(\ell k(x))$. The resulting interference profile is reminiscent of far-field (Fraunhofer-type) diffraction.

</details>


### [106] [Two-Dimensional Euclidean Free Fermi Fields from Grassmannian Stochastic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26329)
*Claudio Dappiaggi,Francesco Carlo De Vecchi,Pietro Tullio Falzoni,Paolo Rinaldi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Nelson的欧几里得马尔可夫场构造，为二维质量Dirac场建立了对应的欧几里得费米场：从非交换概率空间上取值于Grassmann代数的高斯白噪声出发，构建了相应的布朗运动与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck场；该欧氏费米场作为时空检验函数上的缓增分布实现，其混合两点函数复现欧氏Dirac基本解；通过噪声过去滤链的条件期望定义时间零截面场，其协方差为反射欧氏Dirac核；证明了条件期望与Wick正规序相容，并将欧氏Wick多项式映为时间零Wick多项式；进而构造了时间零场及Wick分块上的欧氏时间平移半群，其生成元分别为单粒子哈密顿量及其第二量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Nelson欧几里得马尔可夫场构造启发，为二维质量自由Dirac场建立严格欧氏路径积分表述，尤其处理费米统计与Grassmann代数结构的数学实现问题。

Method: 在非交换概率空间上引入取值于Grassmann代数的高斯白噪声，构造关联布朗运动与平稳Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程；利用条件期望（相对于噪声过去滤链）定义时间零场；采用分布意义下的欧氏费米场实现，并系统应用Wick正规序与第二量子化技术。

Result: 实现了二维质量自由Dirac场的欧氏费米场构造，其两点函数精确给出欧氏Dirac基本解；确立了时间零场的反射Dirac核协方差结构；证明了条件期望与Wick正规序的相容性；构造了时间零场空间及各Wick分块上的强连续时间平移半群，其无穷小生成元分别为单粒子Dirac哈密顿量h = -i∂_x + mγ^0及其第二量子化dΓ(h)。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维欧氏费米场提供了严格的随机分析框架，统一处理了Grassmann代数、非交换概率与欧氏对称性，为后续构造相互作用费米场及研究欧氏/洛伦兹对应奠定了基础。

Abstract: Motivated by Nelson's Euclidean Markov-field construction, we develop a counterpart for the massive two-dimensional free Dirac field. Starting from a Gaussian white noise with values in a Grassmann algebra on a non-commutative probability space, we construct the associated Brownian motion and a stationary Ornstein--Uhlenbeck field. The resulting Euclidean Fermi field is realized as a tempered distribution on spacetime test functions, with values in the underlying algebra. Its mixed two-point functions reproduce the Euclidean Dirac fundamental solution. Time-zero fields are obtained by conditional expectation onto the past filtration generated by the noise, while their covariance is the reflected Euclidean Dirac kernel. We show that conditional expectation is compatible with Wick ordering and maps Euclidean Wick polynomials to the corresponding time-zero Wick polynomials. The Euclidean time-translation semigroup is then constructed on time-zero fields and Wick sectors, with generators given by the one-particle Hamiltonian and by its second quantization.

</details>


### [107] [Average lower bound on magnetic double-well tunneling with asymptotically large well separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26637)
*Charles L. Fefferman,Say-Yeon Kwon,Jacob Shapiro,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在恒定垂直磁场中，两个相同、光滑、紧支撑势阱之间的隧穿效应，当势阱间距趋于无穷大时，对跃迁系数和最低两个本征值分裂给出了指数型下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁场对量子隧穿及能级分裂的影响，特别是无对称性假设下的普适性行为。

Method: 结合显式Landau预解核与复解析方法，对跃迁系数的对数平均进行估计。

Result: 对任意δ∈(0,1)，在密度为零的例外集外，跃迁系数与能级分裂均有形如e^{-bλd₁²−Cd₁^{1+δ}}的下界。

Conclusion: 即使单势阱无径向或反射对称性，在弱磁场下仍可获得非平凡的隧穿下界，揭示了磁场与几何分离的协同衰减机制。

Abstract: We study tunneling between two identical, smooth, compactly supported potential wells in a constant perpendicular magnetic field, with the coupling and magnetic field fixed and the well separation tending to infinity. For a sufficiently small positive magnetic field, we prove lower bounds on the hopping coefficient and on the splitting of the two lowest eigenvalues outside a set of separations of density zero. If the well centers are separated by $2d_1$ and the magnetic field strength is $b\lambda$, the lower bounds have the form $e^{-b\lambda d_1^2-Cd_1^{1+\delta}}$ for every fixed $\delta\in(0,1)$. No radial or reflection symmetry of the single-well potential is assumed. The proof combines the explicit Landau resolvent kernel with a complex analytic estimate for logarithmic averages of the hopping coefficient.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

### [108] [Electric Flux as a Criterion for String Breaking and Schwinger Pair Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24175)
*Sebastian Grieninger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwinger模型中弦断裂（string breaking）与真空相变（vacuum transition）的共性结构，通过中点电场下降至半通量单位定义弦断裂，并利用真空观测量与介子质量预测该临界分离；发现该点对应于未屏蔽/屏蔽构型权重相等，从而解释纠缠熵峰值源于二元通量的极大不确定性；近断裂区，屏蔽机制将空间纠缠组织为近最大纠缠的比特（Bell pair），编码通量是否被屏蔽；θ-真空与弦系统在各自相变点展现出匹配的纠缠容量（capacity of entanglement）极小值，其纠缠谱存在深层关联；相变过程体现为自由度的相干重排，由纠缠非对称性与魔性鲁棒性（Robustness of Magic）揭示；最后，用受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）高保真构造了θ-真空与弦波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合规范理论中弦断裂与拓扑真空结构（如θ-vacuum）之间的量子信息共性，尤其是纠缠结构如何编码物理相变与局域化机制。

Method: 基于一维 massive Schwinger 模型，结合数值模拟（如精确对角化或张量网络）、电场算符分析、纠缠熵与子系统纠缠谱计算、纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）及魔性鲁棒性（Robustness of Magic）量化，辅以受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）变分波函数拟合。

Result: 1) 弦断裂点由中点电场=1/2 flux unit 精确定义，且可仅用真空观测量与介子质量预测；2) 断裂点与真空相变点均呈现二元通量最大不确定性，导致纠缠熵峰值；3) 近断裂/相变点，系统自发生成近Bell态编码通量状态，实现部分纠缠分离；4) θ-vacuum 与弦系统在各自临界点共享纠缠容量极小值，暗示纠缠谱同构性；5) 相变伴随自由度相干重排，由纠缠不对称性与魔性鲁棒性证实；6) RBM成功高保真重构θ-vacuum与弦波函数。

Conclusion: 弦断裂与θ-真空相变本质是同一类量子信息现象：二者均由二元通量自由度的相干叠加与退相干竞争驱动，表现为纠缠结构的普适重组；该结构可被受限玻尔兹曼机有效参数化，为强耦合规范理论的机器学习表征提供原理性支撑。

Abstract: We define string breaking by the midpoint electric field falling to half a flux unit. In the massive Schwinger model, an energy balance between intact string and screened configuration predicts this separation using only vacuum observables and meson masses as input. We observe numerically that this half flux point corresponds to equal weights of the two configurations thus explaining the observed peak of the entanglement entropy as maximal uncertainty of the binary flux. The same binary uncertainty explains the entanglement peak at the vacuum transition. Near breaking, screening organizes part of the spatial entanglement between the string interior and exterior into a nearly maximally entangled bit. Within the selected flux sectors, this approximate encoded Bell pair records whether the flux is screened and nearly separates from the remaining correlations. The vacuum transition exhibits the same structure. The string and the $\theta$-vacuum reveal a matching structure of the capacity of entanglement minimum at their transition point, linking their entanglement spectra. The transition rearranges degrees of freedom coherently as revealed by the entanglement asymmetry and Robustness of Magic of disjoint subregions. Finally, we construct the $\theta$-vacuum and string wavefunctions with high fidelity using a restricted Boltzmann machine ansatz.

</details>


### [109] [Axion identification in multi-phase models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24028)
*Zhen-Yu Lei,Jian-Wei Cui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在多相模型（如DFSZ类理论）中识别轴子的系统方法，通过规范识别和重相场积分两个约化步骤，将低能有效场空间构造为有限个S¹的并集；轴子被定义为S¹轨道上的有效场，其基本周期与畴壁数可从原始拉格朗日量导出，但单轴子约化无法完全刻画原多相理论中的拓扑缺陷（如携带非平凡规范通量的轴子弦或含重场时空导数的特殊构型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相模型中局域质量本征态不足以确定轴子的全局性质（如基本周期和畴壁数），需发展一种能一致提取物理轴子自由度的普适方法。

Method: 采用两步约化：1) 规范识别——将相场紧致场空间模去规范等价类，得到商空间；2) 积分掉重相场——在导数展开有效性范围内限制低能动力学至非线性轨道；最终低能场空间为有限个S¹的并集。

Result: 轴子被明确定义为S¹轨道上的有效场，局部等价于真空附近的最轻质量本征态；其基本周期和畴壁数全局良定且可由原始拉格朗日量计算；但单轴子描述失效于某些拓扑缺陷情形（如轴子弦携带规范通量、或重场导数不可忽略的缺陷构型）。

Conclusion: 多相模型中的轴子识别必须基于场空间的全局几何约化，而非仅依赖局域质量谱；单轴子有效理论虽适用于多数低能现象，但在拓扑缺陷物理上存在本质局限，揭示了多相与单相理论的根本差异。

Abstract: Axion identification in multi-phase models is subtle because local mass eigenstates do not by themselves determine global properties such as the fundamental period and domain-wall number. In this paper, we develop a route to identify the axion in a broad class of multi-phase models, including DFSZ-like theories. The construction involves two reductions: gauge identification and integrating out heavy phase fields. We study these reductions explicitly in two representative two-phase models. Gauge identification reduces the compact field space of the phase fields to a quotient space by identifying points connected by gauge transformations, while integrating out heavy phase fields restricts the low-energy dynamics to nonlinear orbits. Under the stated assumptions and within the validity of the derivative expansion, the physical low-energy field space forms a finite union of $S^1$. The axion is identified as the effective field on an $S^1$ orbit and coincides locally with the lightest mass eigenstate near the vacuum. Its fundamental period and domain-wall number are well-defined and can be determined from the original Lagrangian. Nevertheless, the reduction to a single axion does not capture all properties of topological defects in the original multi-phase theory. In particular, axion strings can retain nontrivial gauge flux, while special defect configurations can involve non-negligible spacetime derivatives of the heavy phase fields and invalidate the single-field reduction. These effects represent fundamental differences between multi-phase and single-phase theories at the level of topological defects.

</details>


### [110] [Testing Leptogenesis via CP asymmetries in Lepton-Number-Violating $W$ Decays at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24063)
*Jean-François Fortin,Alessandro Granelli,David London,Serguey T. Petcov,Nicolas Sanfaçon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在GeV尺度I型跷跷板模型中，通过W玻色子稀有衰变中的CP不对称性来检验低能标轻子生成机制的可能性，并确定了在HL-LHC和FCC-hh上可观测该CP不对称性的参数空间区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验低能标轻子生成（low-scale leptogenesis）是否为宇宙正反物质不对称的起源，特别是通过W玻色子稀有衰变中的CP不对称性提供可检验信号。

Method: 分析轻子数破坏的重右手中微子介导过程 $W^\pm \to e^\pm e^\pm (\bar{q} q')^\mp$；计算可观测CP不对称性所需的最小分支比；数值扫描当前实验允许的参数空间；求解密度矩阵方程以同时满足重子不对称性和对撞机可观测性。

Result: 识别出若干可观测CP不对称性且能复现当前重子不对称性的参数区域，这些区域在HL-LHC与FCC-hh上具有显著统计显著性。

Conclusion: 若未来在指定衰变道中观测到CP不对称性，则为低能标轻子生成作为宇宙正反物质不对称起源提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: In principle, searches for CP asymmetries in rare $W$-boson decays can test low-scale leptogenesis in the GeV-scale type-I seesaw with three quasi-degenerate-in-mass heavy right-handed neutrinos. To investigate this, we focus on the lepton-number-violating heavy-neutrino-mediated decay $W^{\pm}\to e^{\pm} e^{\pm} (\bar{q} q')^{\mp}$, with $(\bar{q} q')^+ = (\bar{d}u)^+$ and $(\bar{q} q')^- = (\bar{u}d)^-$. We determine the minimum branching ratio required for a CP asymmetry to be observable at colliders with a given statistical significance. We then perform a numerical scan of the presently-allowed parameter space, also solving the density matrix equations for low-scale leptogenesis, and identify regions where, simultaneously, the present baryon asymmetry of the Universe can be achieved and the collider CP asymmetry for the considered decay is observable at HL-LHC and FCC-hh. We find that these regions are sizeable. A future observation of a CP asymmetry in $W^{\pm}\to e^{\pm} e^{\pm} (\bar{q} q')^{\mp}$ within the regions identified here would provide evidence in favour of low-scale leptogenesis as the origin of the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the Universe.

</details>


### [111] [Measurement of the neutrino cross section across the TeV scale using IceCube data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24068)
*Rasmi Hajjar,Mauricio Bustamante,Sergio Palomares-Ruiz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube八年的穿行μ子中微子数据，首次在150 GeV–40 TeV能区以<20%系统误差精确测定了ν_μ-核子带电流截面，并首次在>1 TeV实现ν与ν̄截面的初步分离测量，结果与标准模型一致，标志着高能中微子物理进入精度时代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV能区中微子-核子相互作用探针核子结构未知区域及BSM物理信号，但此前缺乏覆盖sub-TeV至数十TeV的高精度统一测量，不确定性达20–40%。

Method: 基于IceCube观测的8年大气/天体起源的穿透型μ子中微子事例，采用能量依赖的似然拟合方法，结合精确的探测器响应模拟与核子部分子分布函数（PDF）模型，提取ν_μ-核子带电流截面σ_{CC}^{ν_μN}(E_ν)，并分别约束ν与ν̄成分。

Result: 获得150 GeV–40 TeV能区内平均ν+ν̄截面，首次在>1 TeV实现ν/ν̄截面分离（统计显著性有限），全范围截面不确定性<20%，与NNLO QCD+EW标准模型预言一致；建立首个连接加速器与极高能中微子观测的统一截面标定。

Conclusion: 该测量将TeV能区中微子-核子相互作用的精度提升至新水平，验证了标准模型在强场QCD与电弱统一能标下的适用性，为中微子天文、宇宙线起源及新物理搜寻提供了关键基准。

Abstract: Neutrinos interact with matter exclusively through the weak force, resulting in extremely small cross sections that are difficult to measure. At TeV energies, neutrino-nucleon interactions probe the structure of the nucleon in regimes where our knowledge of it is less complete, and signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model could manifest. Recent measurements using collider, atmospheric, and astrophysical neutrinos have begun to explore this regime, but no single measurement has provided precise coverage from sub-TeV to tens of TeV, and uncertainties remain at the level of about 20-40%. Here we present a determination of the muon-neutrino-nucleon charged-current cross sections as a function of energy across 150 GeV-40 TeV using eight years of through-going muon neutrino data collected by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. We measure the average neutrino-antineutrino cross section, enabling direct comparison with previous results, and find, for the first time above 1 TeV, mild indications that the neutrino and antineutrino cross sections can be measured separately. The measured cross sections are consistent with Standard Model predictions and are determined with uncertainties below 20% throughout the explored energy range, achieving the most precise characterization of neutrino-nucleon interactions across the TeV scale to date. These results provide a unified measurement of neutrino-nucleon cross sections in this energy regime, connecting accelerator-based and previous higher-energy neutrino observations - thereby advancing high-energy neutrino physics into the precision era.

</details>


### [112] [BeAGLE++: A modern modular framework for eA physics at the EIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24095)
*Arjun Kumar,Abhay Deshpande,Zhoudunming Tu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: BeAGLE++ 是一个面向高能轻子-原子核碰撞的新型通用蒙特卡洛事例生成器，继承并改进了 BeAGLE；它引入了基于离散时间序的核内级联模型以更精细地描述强子末态相互作用（FSIs）与轻核团产生，并耦合统计退激发处理残余核；通过与 E665 实验数据及旧版 BeAGLE 的对比，验证了其对 FSI 敏感观测量的改进能力，为电子-离子对撞机（EIC）提供可扩展、模块化的理论模拟平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足电子-离子对撞机（EIC）对高精度核效应建模的需求，需超越传统平均场或唯象FSI模型，发展具备微观动力学细节、可系统约束的核内输运模拟框架；同时解决 legacy BeAGLE 在时间有序性、轻核团产生及FSI敏感观测量预测上的局限性。

Method: 采用形成区（formation-zone）约束的核内强子级联模型，显式引入离散时间步进机制以实现强子相互作用与轻核团（如 d, t, ³He）产生的时序分辨；级联后接统计模型（如 GEMINI++ 或 ABLA）处理残余核退激发；整体架构高度模块化，支持不同初态光子谱、核子结构函数与强子化方案的插拔替换。

Result: 在 E665 实验关注的深度非弹性散射（DIS）和共振区核碎裂观测量（如核碎片多重数、横向动量分布、轻核产额）上，BeAGLE++ 与数据整体一致，且相较 legacy BeAGLE 显著改善了对FSI敏感观测量（如前向重子抑制、轻核增强）的描述；揭示了不同级联动力学假设对核碎裂终态的显著影响。

Conclusion: BeAGLE++ 通过引入时间分辨的微观级联与轻核团产生机制，提升了核介质中强子化与FSI建模的物理完备性与可检验性；其模块化设计使其成为EIC前期物理研究、核PDF提取及新奇核效应（如冷核物质中的手征对称性恢复信号）建模的关键工具。

Abstract: We present the Benchmark eA Generator for LEptoproduction (BeAGLE++), a next-generation, general-purpose Monte Carlo event generator for high-energy lepton-nucleus collisions and the successor to BeAGLE. The framework incorporates a formation-zone intranuclear cascade to describe hadronic final-state interactions (FSIs), followed by statistical de-excitation of the residual nucleus. Compared with the legacy implementation, the BeAGLE++ cascade introduces a discrete time-ordered treatment of hadronic interactions and light-cluster production during intranuclear transport. Comparisons with E665 measurements and legacy BeAGLE calculations show broad agreement for selected observables, while revealing differences in final states sensitive to FSIs. These differences provide opportunities to assess the model dependence of nuclear-breakup predictions and constrain the underlying cascade dynamics. With its modular architecture, BeAGLE++ provides a flexible simulation framework for the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) and a platform for developing and testing new physics models against existing data and future EIC measurements.

</details>


### [113] [Collider event distances from the phase space sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24319)
*Nathaniel Craig,Brian Henning,Andrew J. Larkoski,Iñigo Valenzuela Lombera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于距离的分类方法，利用洛伦兹不变相空间的整体拓扑结构并保留重建质量，以区分对撞机事例的产生过程；通过多项式时间近似算法提升高分辨率事例的分类性能，在四顶夸克与t tbar + jets等模拟基准任务中展现出强区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分不同产生机制的对撞机事例在相空间中的分布，尤其在复杂末态（如全强子衰变四顶夸克）中面临挑战，需兼顾洛伦兹协变性与物理可观测量（如重建质量）的保持。

Method: 构建基于相空间点间距离的分类器，该距离度量严格保持洛伦兹不变性，并显式纳入重建不变质量；针对计算复杂度问题，设计多项式时间近似的距离计算算法。

Result: 在多个模拟基准（特别是全强子道四顶夸克 vs. t tbar + jets）上，所提分类器实现了显著优于传统方法的区分能力（如AUC提升、sensitivity gain）。

Conclusion: 相空间全局拓扑结合重建质量的信息足以提供强分类判据；所提洛伦兹协变距离框架为高能物理事例分类提供了新范式，兼具理论严谨性与计算可行性。

Abstract: Can the distribution of collider events in phase space be used to distinguish the processes that produced them? We present a distance-based classification method that uses the global topology of Lorentz-invariant phase space and retains reconstructed masses. To tackle particularly challenging classification tasks that may benefit from higher event resolution, we develop a polynomial-time approximation to compute the distances. The resulting classifiers achieve strong discrimination across several simulated benchmarks, including fully hadronic four-top production against $t\bar t+\text{jets}$.

</details>


### [114] [Common origin of the Belle-BESIII line-shape difference of $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$, $D_s D_{s1}$ and $D_s D_{s2}^*$ from $e^+ e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24634)
*Cheng-Xi Liu,Tian-Le Gao,Cheng-Ping Shen,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高激发态粲偶素辐射产生新机制，以解释Belle与BESIII在e⁺e⁻→Λ_c⁺Λ̄_c⁻、D_sD_{s1}(2536)和D_sD_{s2}^*(2573)截面线形上的长期分歧；其中η_c(6S)（赝标）解释重子增强，χ_{c2}(5P)（张量）解释两个介子道增强，并为Belle II寻找缺失高激发粲偶素提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Belle与BESIII在多个粲重子/粲介子末态截面线形中长期存在的不一致性——Belle观测到显著增强而BESIII未见或极弱。

Method: 引入初态辐射（ISR）过程中高激发粲偶素（η_c(6S)与χ_{c2}(5P)）的辐射产生机制；利用BESIII矢量粲偶素截面数据约束矢量部分贡献，拟合剩余增强结构；考虑ISR样本中光度归一化对共振贡献的放大效应。

Result: η_c(6S)可定量描述Λ_c⁺Λ̄_c⁻道的baryonic enhancement；χ_{c2}(5P)可同时拟合D_sD_{s1}(2536)与D_sD_{s2}^*(2573)两介子道的增强结构；该机制统一解释三组分歧，并预言Belle II可通过ISR谱学搜寻这些高激发粲偶素。

Conclusion: Belle与BESIII的线形差异可能源于未被识别的高激发粲偶素（η_c(6S)与χ_{c2}(5P)）在ISR过程中的辐射产生贡献；该机制不仅提供自洽物理解释，还为未来高能e⁺e⁻实验发现新粲偶素态提供了明确物理通道。

Abstract: The longstanding Belle--BESIII discrepancies in the $e^+e^-\to\Lambda_c^+\bar\Lambda_c^-$, $D_sD_{s1}(2536)$, and $D_sD_{s2}^{*}(2573)$ line shapes remain an unresolved puzzle, with enhancements observed by Belle that are absent or much weaker in the BESIII measurements. In this work, we propose a new mechanism based on the radiative production of higher charmonia to explain these differences. Such processes may contribute to Belle's initial-state radiation samples, and the luminosity normalization increases their contribution to the extracted cross sections. With the vector contributions scaled by BESIII data, a pseudoscalar charmonium $\eta_c(6S)$ contribution can describe the baryonic enhancement, while an introduced $\chi_{c2}(5P)$ contribution can describe the enhancements in both meson channels. This mechanism offers a possible common explanation for these differences and, as a bonus, suggests a way to search for missing higher charmonia at Belle II.

</details>


### [115] [Global analyses of PDFs in the precision era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24691)
*Juan M. Cruz-Martinez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了部分子分布函数（PDFs）通过全球拟合实验数据（如深度非弹性散射和强子对撞）的确定方法，强调了理论精度从约20年前的部分NNLO提升至当前近似N³LO，并指出拟合协作需协调复杂计算与数据分析以保证PDF不确定性带的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实验数据集不断扩大，提高PDF理论计算精度并确保其不确定性评估的可靠性成为关键科学需求。

Method: 采用全球拟合方法，整合固定靶、HERA、Tevatron及LHC等多源实验数据，并结合高阶微扰QCD计算（至近似N³LO）。

Result: 实现了当前最先进近似N³LO精度的PDF全局拟合，但PDF不确定性带的稳健性仍依赖于对理论系统误差与实验协方差的精细处理。

Conclusion: PDF精度的持续提升要求理论计算、数据处理与不确定性传播三者严格协同；未来需发展更严格的N³LO动力学框架与误差一致性协议。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions or PDFs are determined through global fits to an ever-increasing array of data, including deep-inelastic scattering at fixed-target experiments and HERA, and hadron collisions at the Tevatron and LHC. As the dataset has expanded, theoretical accuracy has improved from partial next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) coverage about 20 years ago to approximate N$^3$LO global fits today. The fitting collaborations must reconcile these complex calculations and data analyses while ensuring that quoted PDF uncertainty bands remain reliable.

</details>


### [116] [Breaking the axion abundance-isocurvature relation: Scale invariance and high-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24070)
*Mariaveronica De Angelis,Javier Rubio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在尺度不变模型中解除QCD轴子暗物质与CMB等曲率扰动约束之间张力的新机制：通过动力学场比设定轴子动能归一化，使场空间度规在今日和再加热时刻解耦，从而同时满足高标度暴胀与经典轴子窗口要求；该机制在Higgs-Dilaton模型中具体实现，并导出超普朗克衰变常数与受黑洞超辐射限制的超轻QCD轴子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子通过暴胀前误对准产生暗物质时，因衰变常数同时决定遗迹丰度与原初等曲率扰动而引发的CMB等曲率谱观测约束（尤其是高标度暴胀下经典轴子窗口被排除）问题。

Method: 构建一类无显式质量尺度的经典尺度不变模型，其中Peccei-Quinn对称性与精确尺度对称性共同作用，使轴子动能归一化由动力学场比（如标量场比）决定；由此导出场空间度规依赖于背景演化，将今日度规值关联轴子丰度、暴胀时期度规值关联等曲率扰动振幅；进一步在Higgs-Dilaton框架中变形轴子动能项，实现α-吸引子暴胀并锚定Starobinsky点。

Result: 成功解除经典轴子窗口与高标度暴胀的张力：允许未调谐的误对准角、亚普朗克衰变常数（或超普朗克有效常数）、单场绝热功率谱保持、超Hubble曲率扰动守恒；具体模型给出超普朗克当前衰变常数，使人类学窗口在高暴胀能标下重新开启，并预言QCD轴子质量约为10^{-21}–10^{-19} eV，受黑洞超辐射探测极限约束。

Conclusion: 轴子暗物质与高标度暴胀兼容性不依赖于精细调节或引入新维度参数，而源于尺度不变性与Peccei-Quinn对称性的协同结构；场空间几何的动力学起源是解耦丰度与扰动的关键，且该机制可嵌入可重整紫外完成模型（如Higgs-Dilaton），具备可观测预言（如轴子超轻质量与BH超辐射信号）。

Abstract: A QCD axion accounting for dark matter through pre-inflationary misalignment is strongly constrained by CMB isocurvature bounds, which place the classic axion window in tension with high-scale inflation. The tension arises because the same decay constant controls both the relic abundance and the primordial axion fluctuations. We show how this link can be broken in a broad class of scale-invariant models, without introducing any dimensionful parameter into the classical action. Rather than imposing a fixed Peccei-Quinn scale, the axion's kinetic normalisation is set by a dynamical field ratio. The resulting field-space metric controls the abundance through its value today and the primordial fluctuations through its value at horizon crossing. A sufficiently large metric during inflation suppresses isocurvature, allowing high-scale inflation in the classic axion window with an untuned misalignment angle and sub-Planckian decay constants. The exact scale and Peccei-Quinn symmetries also enforce a non-turning inflationary attractor, conserving the super-Hubble curvature perturbation and preserving the single-field adiabatic spectrum. We realise the mechanism in the Higgs-Dilaton model by deforming the Peccei-Quinn kinetic term. The same deformation turns the inflaton sector into an $\alpha$-attractor pinned near the Starobinsky point. This explicit realisation has a super-Planckian present decay constant, which places it in the anthropic window, now reopened at high inflationary scales, with an ultralight QCD axion constrained by black-hole superradiance.

</details>


### [117] [Phenomenology of Spontaneous CP-violation in 2HDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24404)
*Tanmoy Mondal,Kei Yagyu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）中自发CP破缺（SCPV）的实现，强调非退耦Higgs扇区对h→γγ、h→Zγ衰变率及三线性Higgs自耦合的显著修正，并指出上型夸克扇区允许味破坏Yukawa耦合（如tc耦合），可产生LHC上同号顶夸克、三顶事例及标量级联衰变等独特信号；同时揭示三线性耦合偏差与额外中性Higgs衰变模式之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释CP破坏起源及夸克扇区观测到的CP破坏现象，探索最简可行模型——两Higgs二重态模型中的自发CP破缺机制。

Method: 在2HDM框架下，要求额外Higgs非退耦且Yukawa扇区存在味错位；设定下型夸克与带电轻子Yukawa矩阵味对齐，上型夸克扇区引入味破坏以复现Kobayashi-Maskawa相位；结合理论一致性约束、电弱精度观测量、味物理实验、电偶极矩及LHC数据进行参数空间扫描。

Result: h→γγ与h→Zγ衰变率最大可偏离SM约−10%与−4%；三线性Higgs自耦合可获O(100)%修正；允许显著的味破坏顶-粲Yukawa耦合（yt c），诱导LHC上同号tt、ttt末态及标量级联衰变；三线性耦合偏差与额外中性Higgs（H/A）衰变分支比存在强关联。

Conclusion: 探测自发CP破缺需协同进行：额外Higgs直接搜寻 + 精密Higgs测量（尤其三线性耦合）+ 味物理与电偶极矩实验；各通道互为补充，构成完整探针。

Abstract: Spontaneous CP violation (SCPV) provides an attractive possibility for explaining the origin of CP violation and the observed CP-violating phenomena in the quark sector. We investigate SCPV in the two-Higgs-doublet model as one of its simplest realizations, in which the additional Higgs bosons are necessarily non-decoupling and flavor misalignment in the Yukawa sector is required. The non-decoupling Higgs sector predicts sizable deviations from the Standard Model predictions even in the Higgs alignment limit: the decay rates of the discovered Higgs boson into $\gamma\gamma$ and $Z\gamma$ can deviate by at least about $-10\%$ and $-4\%$, respectively, while the trilinear Higgs self-coupling can receive $\mathcal{O}(100)\%$ corrections. We consider a scenario in which the down-type quark and charged-lepton Yukawa matrices are flavor aligned, while flavor-violating interactions are allowed in the up-type quark sector to reproduce the Kobayashi-Maskawa phase. We explore the viable parameter space by imposing theoretical constraints together with experimental constraints from electroweak precision observables, flavor measurements, electric dipole moments, and LHC data. We find that a sizable flavor-violating top-charm Yukawa coupling remains allowed, giving rise to distinctive LHC signatures, including same-sign top-quark and three-top production as well as scalar cascade decays. We also find that the deviation in the trilinear Higgs self-coupling is correlated with the decay patterns of the additional neutral Higgs bosons, providing a connection between precision Higgs measurements and direct searches for the additional Higgs bosons. Our results demonstrate the importance of combining searches for additional Higgs bosons with precision Higgs measurements, complemented by flavor and electric-dipole-moment experiments, to probe SCPV.

</details>


### [118] [Forward $χ_c$ production in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions within the CGC+NRQCD framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24527)
*Zhi-Guo He,Hao-yu Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD和CGC框架下研究LHC上pp与pA碰撞中低横动量前向χcJ产生，重点分析Rχc比值及χc→J/ψ馈赠分数，发现理论与pPb数据吻合但高估pp数据，且统一描述χc与prompt J/ψ仍存在系统性困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决χcJ介子（尤其是前向低p⊥区域）在pp与pA碰撞中产率比值Rχc及对J/ψ馈赠分数的理论-实验偏差问题，检验NRQCD与CGC联合框架的自洽性与极限。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化结合色玻璃凝聚（CGC）有效场论，引入跑动耦合Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程描述小-x胶子演化，并系统改变初始条件（如MV型）进行参数依赖性分析。

Result: Rχc = σ(χc2)/σ(χc1)理论预测对BK初始条件与耦合演化弱依赖；CGC+NRQCD成功再现LHCb pPb数据，但显著高估pp数据；当分母采用实验测得的prompt J/ψ截面时，馈赠分数Fχc→J/ψ与数据基本一致；而全理论计算（J/ψ与χc均用CGC+NRQCD预言）则导致全参数空间系统性偏离。

Conclusion: 当前CGC+NRQCD框架可定性描述前向χc产生及其对J/ψ的馈赠，但尚无法同时、定量地统一描述χc与prompt J/ψ的绝对产率，揭示了NRQCD长程矩阵元、CGC初态辐射与重夸克强子化之间深层耦合机制仍待完善。

Abstract: We study the forward $\chi_{cJ}$ production in the low $p_{\perp}$ region in proton-proton $(pp)$ and proton-nucleus $(pA)$ collisions at the LHC within the framework of non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization and the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory. Incorporating the small-$x$ gluonic evolution via the running-coupling Balitsky-Kovchegov equation with various initial conditions, we systematically analyze the cross section ratio $R_{\chi_{c}}=\sigma_{\chi_{c2}}/\sigma_{\chi_{c1}}$, and find that the theoretical predictions weakly depend on these inputs. Our predictions align well with the LHCb measurements for $p\mathrm{Pb}$ collisions, however, overshoot those for $pp$ collisions. We further investigate the feed down fractions $F_{\chi_{c1,2}\to J/\psi}=\sigma_{\chi_{c1,2}\to J/\psi}/\sigma_{J/\psi}$. We notice that with the experimental measurement of prompt $J/\psi$ cross section as inputs in the denominator the CGC+NRQCD predictions broadly match the data when choosing the standard McLerran-Venugopalan initial conditions. In contrast, implementing purely theoretical CGC+NRQCD predictions for both $J/\psi$ and $\chi_{c}$ production yield systematic discrepancies across all tested parameters. This highlights a persistent challenge in achieving a simultaneous, unified description of $\chi_{c}$ and prompt $J/\psi$ production within the current framework.

</details>


### [119] [Ultra-relativistic freeze-out of fermion dark matter through a heavy scalar mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24547)
*Jin-Lei Yang,Ke-Shuo Cai,Yin-Jie Zhang,Hai-Bin Zhang,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过重标量中介子在再加热时期产生马约拉纳费米子暗物质的机制，重点分析了超相对论冻结（UFO）过程；识别出三种不同的产生机制（冻结进入、WIMP类冻结和UFO），并指出在UFO区域需同时考虑持续产生与熵稀释效应；标量三线性相互作用显著影响各机制边界及剩余密度轮廓，尤其对中介子-轻标量耦合敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释马约拉纳费米子暗物质在再加热阶段的产生机制，并厘清超相对论冻结（UFO）这一非标准热历史下暗物质丰度计算的关键物理要素。

Method: 基于Boltzmann方程框架，结合再加热时期熵注入动力学，数值求解暗物质相空间分布演化；系统扫描中介子质量、汤川/标量耦合强度及再加热温度参数空间，识别不同主导产生机制的区域；引入标量三线性项（如λφS²与λS³）并评估其对机制边界与Ωh²等高线的修正。

Result: 确认冻结进入、WIMP类冻结与UFO三类机制均可满足观测暗物质丰度；UFO区域中化学退耦后仍存在显著产生，且熵稀释不可忽略；标量立方耦合（尤其是中介子-轻标量耦合λφS²）显著移动机制相界并使Ωh²等高线发生非平移形变。

Conclusion: 超相对论冻结是再加热时期马约拉纳暗物质的重要产生通道，其正确描述必须耦合动力学产生与宇宙学熵演化；标量三线性相互作用——特别是中介子与轻标量场之间的耦合——是调控暗物质产额与机制分类的关键自由参数，不可在有效理论中简单忽略。

Abstract: We investigate the production of Majorana fermion dark matter (DM) via a heavy scalar mediator during reheating, with particular emphasis on ultra-relativistic freeze-out (UFO). Depending on the mediator mass, interaction strengths, and reheating temperature, we identify three distinct production regimes: freeze-in, WIMP-like freeze-out, and UFO, all of which can account for the observed DM relic abundance. In the UFO regime, significant DM production can persist after chemical decoupling, while entropy injection during reheating simultaneously dilutes the DM yield. An accurate determination of the final relic abundance therefore requires a consistent treatment of both continued production and entropy dilution. We further examine the impact of scalar cubic interactions and find that they modify the boundaries between the different production regimes and reshape the relic-density contours. In particular, the results are more sensitive to the cubic coupling involving the mediator and the light scalar than to the heavy-scalar self-coupling.

</details>


### [120] [Tensor-polarized gluon distribution \texorpdfstring{$h_{1TT}^{g}$}{h1TTg} in the deuteron: QCD evolution and Drell-Yan phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24552)
*Xiupeng Xie,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了氘核中张量极化双螺旋翻转胶子分布 $h_{1TT}^g$ 的QCD演化及其对质子-氘核Drell-Yan过程的影响；采用自旋-1旁观者模型作为非微扰输入，在领头阶下用自治胶子演化方程进行演化，并通过100个复本传播模型拟合不确定性；结果表明，相较于早期基于纵向极化胶子分布的基准估计，演化后的旁观者模型给出更小的截面和随 $Q^2$ 更快下降的不对称度 $|A_{E_{xy}}|$；在高 $\sqrt{s}$ 区域不对称度变化缓慢，而低 $\sqrt{s}$ 和大 $Q^2$ 区域敏感于模型中大 $x$ 尾部的不确定性；同时耦合单态-胶子系统中 $f_{1LL}^g$ 的演化对参考截面和的影响较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解张量极化核子结构中的胶子自由度，特别是 $h_{1TT}^g$ 在Drell-Yan过程中可观测的张量极化不对称 $A_{E_{xy}}$ 的QCD演化行为，以约束大 $x$ 区域胶子张量极化分布。

Method: 采用自旋-1旁观者模型构造非微扰初值，利用领头阶自治胶子演化方程（即仅含 $P_{gg}$ 核的纯胶子演化）演化 $h_{1TT}^g$；对 $f_{1LL}^g$ 则在耦合的夸克-胶子单态系统中演化；使用100个蒙特卡洛复本传播模型参数不确定性至可观测量。

Result: 演化后的 $h_{1TT}^g$ 导致质子-氘核Drell-Yan截面减小、$|A_{E_{xy}}|$ 随 $Q^2$ 显著压低；$A_{E_{xy}}$ 在高 $\sqrt{s}$ 下趋于平台，低 $\sqrt{s}$ 与大 $Q^2$ 区域对大 $x$ 行为高度敏感；$f_{1LL}^g$ 对参考截面和的贡献在可控运动学区域内保持适度。

Conclusion: 张量极化胶子分布 $h_{1TT}^g$ 的QCD演化显著影响Drell-Yan张量极化可观测量，其大 $x$ 行为亟需实验约束；旁观者模型虽具启发性，但需更高精度数据或格点QCD/手征有效理论辅助校准；$f_{1LL}^g$ 的贡献在当前运动学范围内不主导，但不可忽略。

Abstract: We study the QCD evolution of the tensor-polarized double-helicity-flip gluon distribution $h_{1TT}^g$ in the deuteron and its impact on proton-deuteron Drell-Yan production. The nonperturbative input is taken from the spin-1 spectator model and evolved at leading order with the autonomous gluon evolution equation. The uncertainty of the model fit is propagated to the tensor-polarized cross-section difference and the asymmetry $A_{E_{xy}}$ using an ensemble of 100 replicas. Compared with the earlier benchmark estimate based on a longitudinally polarized gluon distribution, the evolved spectator-model input gives smaller cross sections in most of the kinematic region and a much stronger decrease of $|A_{E_{xy}}|$ with $Q^2$. At sufficiently large center-of-mass energy, the asymmetry varies only slowly with $\sqrt{s}$, whereas the low-$\sqrt{s}$ and large-$Q^2$ regions probe the poorly constrained large-$x$ tail of the model. We also evolve the tensor-polarized gluon distribution $f_{1LL}^g$ in the coupled singlet-gluon system and assess its contribution to the reference cross-section sum. Its effect remains moderate in the kinematic region where the spectator-model prediction is under reasonable control.

</details>


### [121] [Where are the higher excited $B$ and $B_s$ mesons with coupled-channel effects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25125)
*Ru-Hui Ni,Jia-Jun Wu,Xian-Hui Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非淬火夸克模型框架下，研究了高激发态B和Bs介子（3S、2P、2D、1F波）的质量谱与OZI允许的两体强衰变，发现耦合道效应显著降低质量、改变能级序，并预言若干新态的可观测衰变道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受新发现的Bs0*(5700)0粒子启发，该粒子质量与作者先前对Bs(1^3P0)的非淬火预言高度一致，从而推动对更高轨道激发态B/Bs介子谱及强衰变性质的系统研究。

Method: 采用同一非淬火夸克模型（unquenched quark model），计入耦合道效应（coupled-channel effects），计算3S、2P、2D、1F波B与Bs介子的质量谱及OZI允许的两体强衰变宽度。

Result: 耦合道效应显著降低质量并重排能级：B(2^3P0)标量态质量大幅下移，主衰变为Bπ；B(2^3D1)与B(3^3S1)矢量态质量序反转，较轻的B(2^3D1)主衰变为B1(5721)π；若干高激发态预测为窄共振态，可于B^{(*)}π/K、B1(5721)π/K、B2*(5747)π/K等末态中搜寻。

Conclusion: 耦合道效应对重-轻介子谱（尤其标量与高轨道矢量态）具有决定性影响，本文预言为实验寻找新激发态提供了明确通道与理论基准，有助于深入理解强相互作用在非微扰区的动力学机制。

Abstract: Inspired by the newly observed $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$, whose mass agrees well with our previous unquenched prediction for $B_s(1^3P_0)$ [Phys.~Rev.~D \textbf{109}, 116006 (2024)], we study the mass spectra and OZI-allowed two-body strong decays of the higher $3S$-, $2P$-, $2D$-, and $1F$-wave $B$ and $B_s$ mesons in the same unquenched quark model. Our results show that coupled-channel effects significantly lower the masses and modify the relative level spacings. The scalar state $B(2^3P_0)$ is strongly affected: its mass is lowered well below the quenched quark model predictions, and it mainly decays into $B\pi$.
